# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-11-13 through 2026-11-19  
**Updated:** 2026-11-13T10:58:53+00:00  
**Book-ahead watch:** through 2027-03-13

## Best date plans

### 2026-11-14 — Castle in the Sky on 35mm + Cheryl's Global Soul

**Castle in the Sky on 35mm, then a meal.** Global comfort food at Cheryl's Global Soul. It is a 21-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=188+Prospect+Park+West%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=236+Underhill+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/movies/castle-in-the-sky/) · [Event booking](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/purchase/24543407/) · [Restaurant](https://cherylsglobalsoul.com/) · [Reservations](https://cherylsglobalsoul.com/reservations)

### 2026-11-14 — Roebling Rink Opening Day — Free Morning Session + Well Nice

**Roebling Rink Opening Day — Free Morning Session, then a meal.** Wine bar and European comfort food at Well Nice. It is 2.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 10:30 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Emily+Warren+Roebling+Plaza%2C+Brooklyn+Bridge+Park%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=1038+Bedford+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11205&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.roeblingrink.com/) · [Restaurant](https://www.wellnicewinebar.com)

### 2026-11-17 — Union Square Holiday Market 2026 + Carnitas Ramirez

**Union Square Holiday Market 2026, then a meal.** Mexican carnitas at Carnitas Ramirez. It is a 19-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Union+Square+Park%3A+South+Plaza&destination=210+East+3rd+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://urbanspacemarkets.com/pages/union-square) · [Restaurant](https://www.carnitasramirez.com/)

### 2026-11-15 — Brooklyn Flea DUMBO — Sunday Market + Āzar

**Brooklyn Flea DUMBO — Sunday Market, then a meal.** Persian street food at Āzar. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=80+Pearl+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&destination=740+Driggs+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.brooklynflea.com/) · [Restaurant](https://azar.nyc/)

### 2026-11-16 — Ibérico + Bodega Cats of New York: Dan Rimada

**Dinner, then Bodega Cats of New York: Dan Rimada.** Iberian at Ibérico. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Bodega Cats of New York: Dan Rimada starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=258+DeKalb+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11205&destination=28+Adams+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://powerhousearena.com/events/book-launch-bodega-cats-of-new-york-by-dan-rimada/) · [Event booking](https://events.ticketleap.com/tickets/powerhousebookstores/book-launch-bodega-cats-of-new-york-by-dan-rimada) · [Restaurant](https://ibericobk.com/)

### 2026-11-14 — ISCP Fall Open Studios: Saturday + Altbau Bakery

**ISCP Fall Open Studios: Saturday, then a meal.** Bakery and café at Altbau Bakery. It is a 23-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 1 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=1040+Metropolitan+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&destination=184+North+8th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://iscp-nyc.org/event/2026-fall-open-studios) · [Restaurant](https://altbaubakery.com/)

### 2026-11-13 — Subway Swing: A Boogie for the Ages + Café Paulette

**Subway Swing: A Boogie for the Ages, then dinner.** French bistro at Café Paulette. It is a 15-minute walk from the venue. It runs to 9 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Corner+of+Boerum+Place+%26+Schermerhorn+Street&destination=1+South+Elliott+Place%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.nytransitmuseum.org/program/subwayswing26/) · [Restaurant](https://www.cafepaulette.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/cafe-paulette)

### 2026-11-19 — NYC Resistor Public Craft Night + Cheryl's Global Soul

**NYC Resistor Public Craft Night, then dinner.** Global comfort food at Cheryl's Global Soul. It is a 23-minute walk from the venue. It runs to 9:30 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=87+3rd+Ave%2C+4th+Floor%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=236+Underhill+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.nycresistor.com/) · [Event booking](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/public-craft-night-mondays-and-thursdays-tickets-162729866411) · [Restaurant](https://cherylsglobalsoul.com/) · [Reservations](https://cherylsglobalsoul.com/reservations)

## What to do this week

### 2026-11-13

#### [Bryant Park Holiday Shops](https://bryantpark.org/activities/holiday-shops)

11:00 AM · markets · Free entry; purchases extra · Bryant Park · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 67.0  
Urbanspace's seasonal open-air market brings independent crafts, gifts and food vendors to the park's kiosks.
**Timing:** Friday occurrence of the October 28–January 3 market; weekdays 11am–8pm, weekends 10am–8pm.
**Practical note:** NWS forecasts rain Friday, with gusts up to 26 mph and rain likely into the evening. Wear waterproof layers or choose an indoor market.
**Why now:** The 2026 holiday market is operating throughout the discovery window.
[Official](https://bryantpark.org/activities/holiday-shops)

#### [CopyFest Fundraiser: Three Sets, Three Festivals](https://littlefieldnyc.com/event/?wfea_eb_id=2002566206431)

7:00 PM · music · $18.71 displayed ticket total; $15 base · Littlefield · Gowanus · Brooklyn · Score 66.0  
CopyCopy performs Woodstock-, Lollapalooza- and Coachella-inspired cover sets in a benefit for CHiPS. This is one concert, not three separate festivals.
**Practical note:** 21+. Food and drinks sold separately.
**Why now:** A November 13 benefit with three contrasting sets.

12:30 PM · other · $25 public; $15 MAS members · Grand Central Terminal · Midtown East · Manhattan · Score 63.0  
A Municipal Art Society docent explores the terminal's architecture, preservation history and less familiar spaces on a 75–90-minute tour.

**Practical note:** Registration closes one hour before departure; tickets are nonrefundable unless MAS cancels.
**Why now:** A dated Friday tour with the organizer's fall ticket discount.

## Where to eat

NYC dining for November 13, 2026: happy hours, recent openings, tasting menus and Thanksgiving plans, with uncertain prices and availability clearly marked.

Dining research: 2026-11-13T10:40:37+00:00

No Michelin awards are added. GUI's own structured data describes it as Michelin Selected, not Michelin-starred; its linked Michelin page returned no readable content: https://guide.michelin.com/us/en/new-york-state/new-york/restaurant/gui-1207502. Current star status for these candidates was not independently established. Twelve direct follow-up checks were used alongside the supplied captures. No temporary closure dates were established; null closure fields do not guarantee holiday operation.

### Amélie West Village: $1.50 oysters and $26 early-bird meals

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

The current happy-hour menu offers oysters, wine flights and a burger-and-fries or moules-frites meal with a beer or glass of wine.

Price: Oysters $1.50 each; six oysters with a glass of wine $19; wine flights $14; selected cocktails $10; beer $7; early-bird meal with drink $26.  
Schedule: Daily: happy-hour food 4–6pm; happy-hour drinks from opening until 6pm.  
Conditions: Selected drinks and dishes only. Flights become $19 after 6pm. Oyster minimum and reservation requirement are not stated. Tax, gratuity and service-charge treatment are not stated on this menu. No end date is published.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/happy-hour)

### Amélie West Village: $26 two-course weekday lunch

Deals & happy hours — Amélie West Village

A comparatively inexpensive sit-down lunch with choices including chicken paillard, croque monsieur and moules-frites.

Price: Two courses $26; optional glass of wine $10; burger supplement $5; steak frites or salade Niçoise supplement $8.  
Schedule: Monday–Friday, noon–3:30pm.  
Conditions: Tax and gratuity explicitly excluded. One bread basket included; extra basket $3. Other substitutions and additions cost extra. The page labels this two courses but displays appetizer, entrée and dessert sections without explaining permitted combinations. Reservation requirement and end date are not stated.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/wv/menus/bistro)

### Amélie Upper West Side: daily happy hour and Sunday–Monday oyster pricing

Deals & happy hours — Amélie Upper West Side

This branch has its own pricing: selected $9 cocktails, $14 wine flights and $1.50 oysters, with the oyster offer extended all day on Sundays and Mondays.

Price: Selected cocktails $9; Aperol spritz $11; wine flights $14; wine of the day $10/glass or $40/bottle; oysters $1.50 each; early-bird meal with drink $26.  
Schedule: Happy hour daily 4–6pm; $1.50 oysters also all day Sunday and Monday.  
Conditions: $26 options are burger and fries with Kronenbourg 1664 draft, or moules-frites with house white wine. Burger additions $3 each. Wine flights become $18 after 6pm. Oyster minimum, mandatory reservations, tax, gratuity and service charges are not stated. No end date is published.  
[Source](https://ameliewinebar.com/uws/menus/happy-hour)

### GUI: $65 three-course dinner beyond the pre-theater window

Deals & happy hours — GUI Steakhouse & Prime Rib

Despite its pre-theater name, the official menu advertises availability until closing. Choices include hanger steak frites, chicken, fish and vegetarian eggplant.

Price: $65 per person for three courses.  
Schedule: Daily, 4pm–close. Published closing hours: Monday–Saturday 10pm; Sunday 9pm.  
Conditions: Choose one appetizer, main and dessert. Holiday applicability is unconfirmed: Eater separately reports a Thanksgiving prix fixe, so do not assume the $65 menu operates November 26. Reservation requirements, tax, gratuity and service charges are unstated; no end date is published.  
[Source](https://guinyc.com/gui-pre-theater-menu)

### Junoon: $43 two-course or $49 three-course lunch

Deals & happy hours — Junoon

The Lunch Prestige Menu provides a lower-cost introduction to Junoon, with basmati rice and naan included.

Price: Two courses $43; three courses $49.  
Schedule: Lunch; applicable days and exact hours were not established from the readable official pages.  
Conditions: Basmati rice and naan included; separately priced accompaniments are extra. Official menu fine print states a 20% service charge for parties of five or more. Other tax/tip treatment, mandatory reservations and holiday availability are unconfirmed. No end date is published.  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)

### Miss Lily's 7A: $25 brunch beverage add-on

Deals & happy hours — Miss Lily's 7A

The official brunch menu offers unlimited selected drinks with a separately purchased Signature Dish.

Price: $25 per person for the beverage add-on; required Signature Dish costs extra.  
Schedule: Brunch Saturday and Sunday, noon–4pm.  
Conditions: Requires a separately purchased Signature Dish. Selected unlimited drinks are Pure Passion, Rum Punch, Coco-Lemon Drop and One Love Bellini; the duration is unstated. The restaurant's October 5 dinner PDF states an automatic 18% gratuity, but applicability to brunch could not be established because the linked brunch PDF was not readable. Tax and reservation requirements remain unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://v1-misslilys.getbento.com/east-village-7a/)

### Frenzie: late-night pizza-and-drink packages from $40

Deals & happy hours — Frenzie

A walk-in-only pizza and wine bar offering bundled late-night pies with beer or cocktails.

Price: Classic cheese pie with a pitcher of Narragansett $40; with two daiquiris $45; with two martinis $50. Pepperoni adds $4.  
Schedule: Thursday–Saturday after 10pm. Official kitchen closing time on these nights is 1:30am.  
Conditions: Walk-in only. Packages contain the specified pie and drinks; substitution rules are not stated. Takeout eligibility for these packages is unconfirmed. Tax, gratuity and service charges are not stated. No end date is published.  
[Source](https://www.frenzienyc.com/menus/)

The restaurant's Thanksgiving 2026 menu offers three-plus courses, with main-course choices including turkey, halibut, filet au poivre and wild mushroom rice pilaf.

Schedule: Thanksgiving, November 26; holiday seating hours unconfirmed.  
Conditions: Holiday booking is advertised; availability and cancellation terms were not verified. Tax, gratuity and service charges are not stated in the readable menu.
